  def mcnemar_disagreement_table(predictions, eval_set, model_a="original_teacher", model_b="teacher_plus_10participants"):
  a = predictions[(predictions["eval_set"] == eval_set) & (predictions["model"] == model_a)].sort_values("sample_index")
  b = predictions[(predictions["eval_set"] == eval_set) & (predictions["model"] == model_b)].sort_values("sample_index")
  raise ValueError("Prediction lengths differ; cannot compare paired correctness.")
  a_correct = a["correct"].to_numpy(bool)
  b_correct = b["correct"].to_numpy(bool)
  both_correct = int(np.sum(a_correct & b_correct))
  a_only = int(np.sum(a_correct & ~b_correct))
  b_only = int(np.sum(~a_correct & b_correct))
  both_wrong = int(np.sum(~a_correct & ~b_correct))
  denom = a_only + b_only
  "eval_set": eval_set,
  mcnemar_df = pd.DataFrame([
  mcnemar_disagreement_table(predictions_df, eval_set)
  for eval_set in EVAL_SETS.keys()
  pred_original = predictions_df[predictions_df["model"] == "original_teacher"].copy()
  pred_plus10 = predictions_df[predictions_df["model"] == "teacher_plus_10participants"].copy()
  paired_df = pred_original.merge(
  on=["eval_set", "sample_index", "y_true", "y_true_name"],
  suffixes=("_original", "_plus10"),
  disagreements_df = paired_df[paired_df["y_pred_original"] != paired_df["y_pred_plus10"]].copy()

  sgkf = StratifiedGroupKFold(n_splits=10, shuffle=True, random_state=SEED)

CELL 18
  def downsample_60hz_to_10hz(df, feature_cols):
  df = df[feature_cols].apply(pd.to_numeric, errors="coerce")
  df = df.replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan)
  df = df.ffill().bfill().fillna(0.0)
  usable_rows = (len(df) // DOWNSAMPLE_FACTOR) * DOWNSAMPLE_FACTOR
  df = df.iloc[:usable_rows]
  df_10hz = (
  df.groupby(np.arange(len(df)) // DOWNSAMPLE_FACTOR)
  recording_mean = df_10hz.mean()
  df_10hz = (df_10hz - recording_mean).abs()
  df_10hz = df_10hz / (df_10hz.max() + 1e-8)
  return df_10hz
CELL 20
  def student_style_keep_starts(
  df_10hz,
  seq_length=SEQ_LENGTH,
  threshold=STUDENT_THRESHOLD,
  use_threshold=USE_STUDENT_THRESHOLD,
  if not use_threshold:

  def downsample_60hz_to_10hz_numeric(df, feature_cols):
  missing = [col for col in feature_cols if col not in df.columns]
  raise ValueError(f"Missing required columns: {missing[:10]} total={len(missing)}")
  features = df[feature_cols].apply(pd.to_numeric, errors="coerce")
  features = features.replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan)
  features = features.ffill().bfill().fillna(0.0)
  usable_rows = (len(features) // DOWNSAMPLE_FACTOR) * DOWNSAMPLE_FACTOR
  features = features.iloc[:usable_rows]
  .groupby(np.arange(len(features)) // DOWNSAMPLE_FACTOR)

  def soften_probabilities(probabilities, temperature=1.0):
  probabilities = np.asarray(probabilities, dtype=np.float32)
  probabilities = np.clip(probabilities, 1e-8, 1.0)
  if temperature == 1.0:
  softened = probabilities
  logits = np.log(probabilities) / float(temperature)
  logits = logits - logits.max(axis=1, keepdims=True)
  softened = np.exp(logits)
  softened = softened / softened.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)
  class Top2Gate(layers.Layer):
  def __init__(self, num_experts=3, gate_hidden=128, tau_init=2.0,
  lb_coef=0.02, ent_coef=0.01, name="gate", **kwargs):
  self.num_experts = num_experts
  self.gate_hidden = gate_hidden
  self.tau = tf.Variable(tau_init, dtype=tf.float32, trainable=False, name=f"{name}_tau")
  self.d1 = layers.Dense(gate_hidden, activation="relu", name=f"{name}_dense1")
  self.d2 = layers.Dense(num_experts, activation=None, name=f"{name}_logits")
  def call(self, gate_in, training=None):
  logits = self.d2(self.d1(gate_in))
  w_soft = tf.nn.softmax(logits / self.tau, axis=-1)
  idx = top2.indices
  vals = tf.nn.softmax(top2.values, axis=-1)
  batch_size = tf.shape(w_soft)[0]
  row_ids = tf.repeat(tf.range(batch_size), repeats=2)
  col_ids = tf.reshape(idx, (-1,))
  scatter_idx = tf.stack([row_ids, col_ids], axis=1)
  scatter_vals = tf.reshape(vals, (-1,))
  w_sparse = tf.scatter_nd(scatter_idx, scatter_vals, (batch_size, self.num_experts))
  mean_usage = tf.reduce_mean(w_sparse, axis=0)
  uniform = tf.fill([self.num_experts], 1.0 / tf.cast(self.num_experts, tf.float32))
  lb_loss = tf.reduce_sum(tf.square(mean_usage - uniform))
  ent = -tf.reduce_mean(
  tf.reduce_sum(w_soft * tf.math.log(tf.clip_by_value(w_soft, 1e-8, 1.0)), axis=-1)
  def get_config(self):
  config = super().get_config()
  "num_experts": self.num_experts,
  "gate_hidden": self.gate_hidden,
  def _teacher_tcn_block(x, filters, kernel_size, dilation_rate, dropout=0.2, name=None):
  res = x
  y = layers.Conv1D(
  padding="causal",
  dilation_rate=dilation_rate,
  name=None if name is None else name + "_c1",
  y = layers.BatchNormalization(name=None if name is None else name + "_bn1")(y)
  y = layers.ReLU(name=None if name is None else name + "_relu1")(y)
  y = layers.Dropout(dropout, name=None if name is None else name + "_do1")(y)
  y = layers.Conv1D(
  padding="causal",
  dilation_rate=dilation_rate,
  name=None if name is None else name + "_c2",
  y = layers.BatchNormalization(name=None if name is None else name + "_bn2")(y)
  res = layers.Conv1D(filters, 1, padding="same", name=None if name is None else name + "_proj")(res)
  res = layers.BatchNormalization(name=None if name is None else name + "_proj_bn")(res)
  y = layers.Add(name=None if name is None else name + "_add")([y, res])
  def _teacher_expert_tcn(x, name_prefix):
  y = _teacher_tcn_block(x, 128, 3, 1, 0.2, name=name_prefix + "_tcn1")
  y = _teacher_tcn_block(y, 128, 3, 2, 0.2, name=name_prefix + "_tcn2")
  y = _teacher_tcn_block(y, 128, 3, 4, 0.2, name=name_prefix + "_tcn3")

  gate_in = layers.GlobalAveragePooling1D(name="gate_pool")(seq_in)

  def infer_best_teacher_column_mapping(y_soft, y_true):

  def tcn_expert(input_tensor, name, units=128, dropout=0.2):
  x = layers.Conv1D(units, 3, padding="causal", dilation_rate=1, activation="relu", name=f"{name}_conv1")(input_tensor)
  x = layers.BatchNormalization(name=f"{name}_bn1")(x)
  x = layers.Dropout(dropout, name=f"{name}_drop1")(x)
  x = layers.Conv1D(units, 3, padding="causal", dilation_rate=2, activation="relu", name=f"{name}_conv2")(x)
  x = layers.BatchNormalization(name=f"{name}_bn2")(x)
  x = layers.Conv1D(units, 3, padding="causal", dilation_rate=4, activation="relu", name=f"{name}_conv3")(x)
  x = layers.GlobalAveragePooling1D(name=f"{name}_pool")(x)
  return layers.Dense(units, activation="relu", name=f"{name}_repr")(x)
  def classifier_from_repr(repr_tensor, name, hidden=256, dropout=0.3):
  x = layers.Dense(hidden, activation="relu", name=f"{name}_hidden")(repr_tensor)
  x = layers.Dropout(dropout, name=f"{name}_dropout")(x)
  return layers.Dense(len(le.classes_), activation="softmax", name=f"{name}_probs")(x)
  def duplicate_outputs(main_probs, include_hard=True, prefix=""):
  outputs = {"soft_output": layers.Lambda(lambda z: z, name=f"{prefix}soft_output")(main_probs)}
  def callbacks_for(name, monitor="val_soft_output_accuracy", checkpoint_dir=None):
  checkpoint_dir = RQ2_CHECKPOINT_DIR if checkpoint_dir is None else Path(checkpoint_dir)
  ckpt_path = checkpoint_dir / f"{name}.best.keras"
  monitor_mode = "max" if "accuracy" in monitor or "f1" in monitor else "min"
  monitor=monitor,
  mode=monitor_mode,
  patience=8,
  restore_best_weights=True,
  monitor=monitor,
  mode=monitor_mode,
  save_best_only=True,
  def json_ready(value):
  if isinstance(value, dict):
  return {str(k): json_ready(v) for k, v in value.items()}
  if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
  return [json_ready(v) for v in value]
  if isinstance(value, np.ndarray):
  return value.tolist()
  if isinstance(value, (np.integer,)):
  return int(value)
  if isinstance(value, (np.floating,)):
  return float(value)
  if isinstance(value, (np.bool_,)):
  return bool(value)
  if isinstance(value, Path):
  return str(value)

  def build_coarse_domain_moe_model():
  flat_input = layers.Input(shape=(SEQ_LENGTH, len(STUDENT_COLS)), name="flat_student_input")
  domain_input = layers.Input(shape=(), dtype=tf.int32, name="domain_input")
  natural_expert = tcn_expert(flat_input, "natural_domain_expert")
  acted_expert = tcn_expert(flat_input, "acted_domain_expert")
  shared_expert = tcn_expert(flat_input, "shared_domain_expert")
  expert_stack = layers.Concatenate(axis=1, name="domain_expert_stack")([
  layers.Reshape((1, 128), name="natural_expert_axis")(natural_expert),
  layers.Reshape((1, 128), name="acted_expert_axis")(acted_expert),
  layers.Reshape((1, 128), name="shared_expert_axis")(shared_expert),
  domain_one_hot = layers.Lambda(
  name="domain_one_hot",
  gate = layers.Dense(16, activation="relu", name="domain_gate_hidden")(domain_one_hot)
  gate = layers.Dense(3, activation="softmax", name="domain_gate")(gate)
  fused = layers.Dot(axes=(1, 1), name="domain_weighted_experts")([gate, expert_stack])
  probs = classifier_from_repr(fused, "coarse_moe_classifier")
  name="rq2_coarse_domain_moe",
  coarse_preview = build_coarse_domain_moe_model()

  def build_emoe_unimodal_distillation_model():
  eye_input = layers.Input(shape=(SEQ_LENGTH, len(EYE_COLS)), name="eye_input")
  head_input = layers.Input(shape=(SEQ_LENGTH, len(HEAD_COLS)), name="head_input")
  hand_input = layers.Input(shape=(SEQ_LENGTH, len(HAND_COLS)), name="hand_input")
  eye_repr = tcn_expert(eye_input, "eye_expert")
  head_repr = tcn_expert(head_input, "head_expert")
  hand_repr = tcn_expert(hand_input, "hand_expert")
  expert_stack = layers.Concatenate(axis=1, name="modality_expert_stack")([
  layers.Reshape((1, 128), name="eye_expert_axis")(eye_repr),
  layers.Reshape((1, 128), name="head_expert_axis")(head_repr),
  layers.Reshape((1, 128), name="hand_expert_axis")(hand_repr),
  router_input = layers.Concatenate(name="sample_conditional_router_input")([eye_repr, head_repr, hand_repr])
  router = layers.Dense(128, activation="relu", name="sample_router_hidden")(router_input)
  router = layers.Dense(3, activation="softmax", name="sample_conditional_router")(router)
  fused = layers.Dot(axes=(1, 1), name="emoe_weighted_experts")([router, expert_stack])
  main_probs = classifier_from_repr(fused, "emoe_classifier")
  outputs = duplicate_outputs(main_probs, include_hard=True)
  name="rq2_emoe_unimodal_distillation",
  emoe_preview = build_emoe_unimodal_distillation_model()
  emoe_preview.summary()

  def json_ready(value):
  if isinstance(value, dict):
  return {str(k): json_ready(v) for k, v in value.items()}
  if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
  return [json_ready(v) for v in value]
  if isinstance(value, np.ndarray):
  return value.tolist()
  if isinstance(value, (np.integer,)):
  return int(value)
  if isinstance(value, (np.floating,)):
  return float(value)
  if isinstance(value, (np.bool_,)):
  return bool(value)
  if isinstance(value, Path):
  return str(value)
  return value

  def downsample_60hz_to_10hz(df: pd.DataFrame, feature_cols: list[str]) -> pd.DataFrame:
  missing = [col for col in feature_cols if col not in df.columns]
  raise ValueError(f"Missing required columns: {missing}")
  features = df[feature_cols].apply(pd.to_numeric, errors="coerce")
  features = features.replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan)
  features = features.ffill().bfill().fillna(0.0)
  usable_rows = (len(features) // DOWNSAMPLE_FACTOR) * DOWNSAMPLE_FACTOR
  features = features.iloc[:usable_rows]
  downsampled = (
  .groupby(np.arange(len(features)) // DOWNSAMPLE_FACTOR)
  def make_student_windows(df_10hz: pd.DataFrame, yemo: int, ydom: int, seq_length: int = SEQ_LENGTH):

  def build_temporal_expert(input_tensor, name: str, units: int = 64, dropout: float = 0.25):
  x = layers.Masking(mask_value=0.0, name=f"{name}_mask")(input_tensor)
  x = layers.Conv1D(units, kernel_size=3, padding="same", activation="relu", name=f"{name}_conv1")(x)
  x = layers.BatchNormalization(name=f"{name}_bn1")(x)
  x = layers.Conv1D(units, kernel_size=3, padding="same", activation="relu", name=f"{name}_conv2")(x)
  x = layers.GlobalAveragePooling1D(name=f"{name}_pool")(x)
  x = layers.Dropout(dropout, name=f"{name}_dropout")(x)
  x = layers.Dense(units, activation="relu", name=f"{name}_dense")(x)
  def build_student_moe(seq_length=SEQ_LENGTH, num_classes=len(le.classes_)):
  eye_input = layers.Input(shape=(seq_length, len(EYE_COLS)), name="eye_input")
  head_input = layers.Input(shape=(seq_length, len(HEAD_COLS)), name="head_input")
  hand_input = layers.Input(shape=(seq_length, len(HAND_COLS)), name="hand_input")
  eye_expert = build_temporal_expert(eye_input, "eye_expert")
  head_expert = build_temporal_expert(head_input, "head_expert")
  hand_expert = build_temporal_expert(hand_input, "hand_expert")
  merged_sequence = layers.Concatenate(axis=-1, name="student_signal_sequence")(
  shared_expert = build_temporal_expert(merged_sequence, "shared_expert")
  experts = layers.Concatenate(axis=1, name="expert_stack")([
  layers.Reshape((1, 64), name="eye_expert_axis")(eye_expert),
  layers.Reshape((1, 64), name="head_expert_axis")(head_expert),
  layers.Reshape((1, 64), name="hand_expert_axis")(hand_expert),
  layers.Reshape((1, 64), name="shared_expert_axis")(shared_expert),
  gate_features = layers.Concatenate(name="gate_features")(
  [eye_expert, head_expert, hand_expert, shared_expert]
  gate = layers.Dense(64, activation="relu", name="gate_hidden")(gate_features)
  gate = layers.Dense(4, activation="softmax", name="expert_gate")(gate)
  moe = layers.Dot(axes=(1, 1), name="moe_weighted_sum")([gate, experts])
  moe = layers.Dropout(0.3, name="moe_dropout")(moe)
  moe = layers.Dense(128, activation="relu", name="classifier_hidden")(moe)
  emotion_output = layers.Dense(num_classes, activation="softmax", name="emotion_output")(moe)
  model = models.Model(
  inputs=[eye_input, head_input, hand_input],
  outputs=emotion_output,
  name="student_MoE_eye_head_hand",
  optimizer=tf.keras.optimizers.Adam(learning_rate=1e-3),
  loss="sparse_categorical_crossentropy",
  metrics=["accuracy"],
  student_model = build_student_moe()
  student_model.summary()

CELL 29
  def make_participant_exclusive_split(record_df, domain_name, n_splits=10, random_state=SEED):
  """Return train/val/test DataFrames for one domain only."""
  record_df = record_df.reset_index(drop=True).copy()
  sgkf = StratifiedGroupKFold(n_splits=n_splits, shuffle=True, random_state=random_state)
  sgkf.split(record_df["path"], record_df["Yemo"], groups=record_df["participant"])
  train_df = record_df[record_df["fold"].between(0, 6)].reset_index(drop=True)
  val_df = record_df[record_df["fold"].between(7, 8)].reset_index(drop=True)
  test_df = record_df[record_df["fold"] == 9].reset_index(drop=True)
  print(f"\n[{domain_name}] participant-exclusive split")
  print("Train participants:", sorted(train_df["participant"].unique()))
  print("Val participants:", sorted(val_df["participant"].unique()))
  print("Test participants:", sorted(test_df["participant"].unique()))
  print("Train participant count:", train_df["participant"].nunique())
  print("Val participant count:", val_df["participant"].nunique())
  print("Test participant count:", test_df["participant"].nunique())
  assert set(train_df["participant"]).isdisjoint(val_df["participant"])
  assert set(train_df["participant"]).isdisjoint(test_df["participant"])
  assert set(val_df["participant"]).isdisjoint(test_df["participant"])
  print("Train distribution:")
  display(pd.crosstab(train_df["participant"], train_df["emotion"]))
  print("Validation distribution:")
  display(pd.crosstab(val_df["participant"], val_df["emotion"]))
  print("Test distribution:")
  display(pd.crosstab(test_df["participant"], test_df["emotion"]))
  return {"all": record_df, "train": train_df, "val": val_df, "test": test_df}

CELL 31
  def downsample_60hz_to_10hz(df, feature_cols):
  df = df[feature_cols].apply(pd.to_numeric, errors="coerce")
  df = df.replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan)
  df = df.ffill().bfill().fillna(0.0)
  usable_rows = (len(df) // DOWNSAMPLE_FACTOR) * DOWNSAMPLE_FACTOR
  df = df.iloc[:usable_rows]
  df_10hz = (
  df.groupby(np.arange(len(df)) // DOWNSAMPLE_FACTOR)
  recording_mean = df_10hz.mean()
  df_10hz = (df_10hz - recording_mean).abs()
  df_10hz = df_10hz / (df_10hz.max() + 1e-8)
  return df_10hz

CELL 68
  # ---- TCN expert blocks ----
  def _tcn_block(x, filters, kernel_size, dilation_rate, dropout=0.2, name=None):
  res = x
  y = layers.Conv1D(filters, kernel_size, padding="causal",
  dilation_rate=dilation_rate, name=None if name is None else name+"_c1")(x)
  y = layers.BatchNormalization(name=None if name is None else name+"_bn1")(y)
  y = layers.ReLU(name=None if name is None else name+"_relu1")(y)
  y = layers.Dropout(dropout, name=None if name is None else name+"_do1")(y)
  y = layers.Conv1D(filters, kernel_size, padding="causal",
  dilation_rate=dilation_rate, name=None if name is None else name+"_c2")(y)
  y = layers.BatchNormalization(name=None if name is None else name+"_bn2")(y)
  res = layers.Conv1D(filters, 1, padding="same", name=None if name is None else name+"_proj")(res)
  res = layers.BatchNormalization(name=None if name is None else name+"_proj_bn")(res)
  y = layers.Add(name=None if name is None else name+"_add")([y, res])
  y = layers.ReLU(name=None if name is None else name+"_relu2")(y)
  def build_expert_tcn(x, name_prefix="exp"):
  y = _tcn_block(x, 128, 3, 1, 0.2, name=name_prefix+"_tcn1")
  y = _tcn_block(y, 128, 3, 2, 0.2, name=name_prefix+"_tcn2")
  y = _tcn_block(y, 128, 3, 4, 0.2, name=name_prefix+"_tcn3")
  y = layers.GlobalAveragePooling1D(name=name_prefix+"_gap")(y)
  class Top2Gate(layers.Layer):
  def __init__(self, num_experts=3, gate_hidden=128, tau_init=2.0,
  lb_coef=0.02, ent_coef=0.01, name="gate", **kwargs):
  self.num_experts = num_experts
  self.gate_hidden = gate_hidden
  self.tau = tf.Variable(tau_init, dtype=tf.float32, trainable=False, name=f"{name}_tau")
  self.d1 = layers.Dense(gate_hidden, activation="relu", name=f"{name}_dense1")
  self.d2 = layers.Dense(num_experts, activation=None, name=f"{name}_logits")
  def call(self, gate_in, training=None):
  logits = self.d2(self.d1(gate_in))
  w_soft = tf.nn.softmax(logits / self.tau, -1)
  idx = top2.indices
  vals = tf.nn.softmax(top2.values, -1)
  B = tf.shape(w_soft)[0]
  row_ids = tf.repeat(tf.range(B), repeats=2)
  col_ids = tf.reshape(idx, (-1,))
  scatter_idx = tf.stack([row_ids, col_ids], axis=1)
  scatter_vals = tf.reshape(vals, (-1,))
  w_sparse = tf.scatter_nd(scatter_idx, scatter_vals, (B, self.num_experts))
  mean_usage = tf.reduce_mean(w_sparse, axis=0)
  uniform = tf.fill([self.num_experts], 1.0 / tf.cast(self.num_experts, tf.float32))
  lb_loss = tf.reduce_sum(tf.square(mean_usage - uniform))
  ent = -tf.reduce_mean(tf.reduce_sum(w_soft * tf.math.log(tf.clip_by_value(w_soft, 1e-8, 1.0)), axis=-1))
  class GateTemperatureScheduler(callbacks.Callback):
  def __init__(self, gate_layer, start_tau=2.0, end_tau=0.7, anneal_epochs=20):
  self.gate = gate_layer
  self.anneal_epochs = max(1, int(anneal_epochs))
  def on_epoch_begin(self, epoch, logs=None):
  p = min(1.0, epoch / self.anneal_epochs)
  tau = self.start_tau + p * (self.end_tau - self.start_tau)
  self.gate.tau.assign(tau)
  K.set_value(self.gate.tau, tau)
  if epoch % 5 == 0:
  print(f"[GateTemperatureScheduler] epoch={epoch} tau={tau:.3f}")
  # ---- Same teacher single-input MoE builder, adapted only by model name ----
  def build_moe_single_input(
  tau_init=2.0, tau_final=0.7,
  gate_hidden=128, classifier_hidden=256,
  lb_coef=0.02, ent_coef=0.01,
  name="Student_MoE_SingleInput_3experts_Top2Gate"
  seq_in = layers.Input(shape=input_shape, name="seq")
  exp_nat = build_expert_tcn(seq_in, "exp_nat")
  exp_act = build_expert_tcn(seq_in, "exp_act")
  exp_shared = build_expert_tcn(seq_in, "exp_shared")
  E_stack = layers.Concatenate(axis=1, name="experts_stack")([e1, e2, e3])
  gate_in = layers.GlobalAveragePooling1D(name="gate_pool")(seq_in)
  gate_layer = Top2Gate(num_experts=3, gate_hidden=gate_hidden,
  tau_init=tau_init, lb_coef=lb_coef, ent_coef=ent_coef, name="gate")
  w_sparse, w_soft = gate_layer(gate_in)
  w_expanded = layers.Lambda(lambda z: tf.expand_dims(z, -1), name="w_expand")(w_sparse)

  def downsample_60hz_to_10hz_numeric(df, feature_cols):
  missing = [col for col in feature_cols if col not in df.columns]
  raise ValueError(f"Missing required columns: {missing[:10]} total={len(missing)}")
  features = df[feature_cols].apply(pd.to_numeric, errors="coerce")
  features = features.replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan)
  features = features.ffill().bfill().fillna(0.0)
  usable_rows = (len(features) // DOWNSAMPLE_FACTOR) * DOWNSAMPLE_FACTOR
  features = features.iloc[:usable_rows]
  .groupby(np.arange(len(features)) // DOWNSAMPLE_FACTOR)
  def make_aligned_student_teacher_windows(row):

CELL 102
  def soften_probabilities(probabilities, temperature=1.0):
  probabilities = np.asarray(probabilities, dtype=np.float32)
  probabilities = np.clip(probabilities, 1e-8, 1.0)
  if temperature == 1.0:
  softened = probabilities
  logits = np.log(probabilities) / float(temperature)
  logits = logits - logits.max(axis=1, keepdims=True)
  softened = np.exp(logits)
  softened = softened / softened.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)
  class Top2Gate(layers.Layer):
  def __init__(self, num_experts=3, gate_hidden=128, tau_init=2.0,
  lb_coef=0.02, ent_coef=0.01, name="gate", **kwargs):
  self.num_experts = num_experts
  self.gate_hidden = gate_hidden
  self.tau = tf.Variable(tau_init, dtype=tf.float32, trainable=False, name=f"{name}_tau")
  self.d1 = layers.Dense(gate_hidden, activation="relu", name=f"{name}_dense1")
  self.d2 = layers.Dense(num_experts, activation=None, name=f"{name}_logits")
  def call(self, gate_in, training=None):
  logits = self.d2(self.d1(gate_in))
  w_soft = tf.nn.softmax(logits / self.tau, axis=-1)
  idx = top2.indices
  vals = tf.nn.softmax(top2.values, axis=-1)
  batch_size = tf.shape(w_soft)[0]
  row_ids = tf.repeat(tf.range(batch_size), repeats=2)
  col_ids = tf.reshape(idx, (-1,))
  scatter_idx = tf.stack([row_ids, col_ids], axis=1)
  scatter_vals = tf.reshape(vals, (-1,))
  w_sparse = tf.scatter_nd(scatter_idx, scatter_vals, (batch_size, self.num_experts))
  mean_usage = tf.reduce_mean(w_sparse, axis=0)
  uniform = tf.fill([self.num_experts], 1.0 / tf.cast(self.num_experts, tf.float32))
  lb_loss = tf.reduce_sum(tf.square(mean_usage - uniform))
  ent = -tf.reduce_mean(
  tf.reduce_sum(w_soft * tf.math.log(tf.clip_by_value(w_soft, 1e-8, 1.0)), axis=-1)
  def get_config(self):
  config = super().get_config()
  "num_experts": self.num_experts,
  "gate_hidden": self.gate_hidden,
  def _teacher_tcn_block(x, filters, kernel_size, dilation_rate, dropout=0.2, name=None):
  res = x
  y = layers.Conv1D(
  padding="causal",
  dilation_rate=dilation_rate,
  name=None if name is None else name + "_c1",
  y = layers.BatchNormalization(name=None if name is None else name + "_bn1")(y)
  y = layers.ReLU(name=None if name is None else name + "_relu1")(y)
  y = layers.Dropout(dropout, name=None if name is None else name + "_do1")(y)
  y = layers.Conv1D(
  padding="causal",
  dilation_rate=dilation_rate,
  name=None if name is None else name + "_c2",
  y = layers.BatchNormalization(name=None if name is None else name + "_bn2")(y)
  res = layers.Conv1D(filters, 1, padding="same", name=None if name is None else name + "_proj")(res)
  res = layers.BatchNormalization(name=None if name is None else name + "_proj_bn")(res)
  y = layers.Add(name=None if name is None else name + "_add")([y, res])

  def infer_best_teacher_column_mapping(y_soft, y_true):
  pred = np.argmax(y_soft, axis=1)
  num_classes = len(le.classes_)
  confusion = confusion_matrix(y_true, pred, labels=np.arange(num_classes))
  best_perm = None
  best_correct = -1
  correct = sum(confusion[perm[col], col] for col in range(num_classes))
  best_correct = correct
  best_perm = perm
  mapping = {col: best_perm[col] for col in range(num_classes)}
  mapped_pred = np.array([mapping[col] for col in pred])
  def apply_teacher_column_mapping(y_soft, mapping):
  remapped = np.zeros_like(y_soft)
  for teacher_col, hard_label_col in mapping.items():
  remapped[:, hard_label_col] = y_soft[:, teacher_col]
  def evaluate_teacher_mapping(split_name, y_soft, y_true, mapping):

  pred = np.argmax(remapped, axis=1)

  def evaluate_ablation(model, inputs, y_true, label):
  probs = model.predict(inputs, verbose=0)

  probs = probs[0]
  y_pred = np.argmax(probs, axis=1)

  def tcn_expert(input_tensor, name, units=128, dropout=0.2):
  x = layers.Conv1D(units, 3, padding="causal", dilation_rate=1, activation="relu", name=f"{name}_conv1")(input_tensor)
  x = layers.BatchNormalization(name=f"{name}_bn1")(x)
  x = layers.Dropout(dropout, name=f"{name}_drop1")(x)
  x = layers.Conv1D(units, 3, padding="causal", dilation_rate=2, activation="relu", name=f"{name}_conv2")(x)
  x = layers.BatchNormalization(name=f"{name}_bn2")(x)
  x = layers.Conv1D(units, 3, padding="causal", dilation_rate=4, activation="relu", name=f"{name}_conv3")(x)
  x = layers.GlobalAveragePooling1D(name=f"{name}_pool")(x)
  return layers.Dense(units, activation="relu", name=f"{name}_repr")(x)
  def classifier_from_repr(repr_tensor, name, hidden=256, dropout=0.3):
  x = layers.Dense(hidden, activation="relu", name=f"{name}_hidden")(repr_tensor)
  x = layers.Dropout(dropout, name=f"{name}_dropout")(x)
  return layers.Dense(len(le.classes_), activation="softmax", name=f"{name}_probs")(x)
  def duplicate_outputs(main_probs, include_hard=True, prefix=""):
  outputs = {"soft_output": layers.Lambda(lambda z: z, name=f"{prefix}soft_output")(main_probs)}
  def callbacks_for(name, monitor="val_soft_output_accuracy", checkpoint_dir=None):
  checkpoint_dir = RQ2_CHECKPOINT_DIR if checkpoint_dir is None else Path(checkpoint_dir)
  ckpt_path = checkpoint_dir / f"{name}.best.keras"
  monitor_mode = "max" if "accuracy" in monitor or "f1" in monitor else "min"
  monitor=monitor,
  mode=monitor_mode,
  patience=8,
  restore_best_weights=True,
  monitor=monitor,
  mode=monitor_mode,
  save_best_only=True,
  def json_ready(value):
  if isinstance(value, dict):
  return {str(k): json_ready(v) for k, v in value.items()}
  if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
  return [json_ready(v) for v in value]
  if isinstance(value, np.ndarray):
  return value.tolist()
  if isinstance(value, (np.integer,)):
  return int(value)
  if isinstance(value, (np.floating,)):
  return float(value)
  if isinstance(value, (np.bool_,)):
  return bool(value)
  if isinstance(value, Path):
  return str(value)

  def build_coarse_domain_moe_model():

  layers.Reshape((1, 128), name="natural_expert_axis")(natural_expert),

  probs = classifier_from_repr(fused, "coarse_moe_classifier")
  name="rq2_coarse_domain_moe",
  coarse_preview = build_coarse_domain_moe_model()

  def build_emoe_unimodal_distillation_model():
  eye_input = layers.Input(shape=(SEQ_LENGTH, len(EYE_COLS)), name="eye_input")
  head_input = layers.Input(shape=(SEQ_LENGTH, len(HEAD_COLS)), name="head_input")
  hand_input = layers.Input(shape=(SEQ_LENGTH, len(HAND_COLS)), name="hand_input")
  eye_repr = tcn_expert(eye_input, "eye_expert")
  head_repr = tcn_expert(head_input, "head_expert")
  hand_repr = tcn_expert(hand_input, "hand_expert")
  expert_stack = layers.Concatenate(axis=1, name="modality_expert_stack")([
  layers.Reshape((1, 128), name="eye_expert_axis")(eye_repr),
  layers.Reshape((1, 128), name="head_expert_axis")(head_repr),
  layers.Reshape((1, 128), name="hand_expert_axis")(hand_repr),
  router_input = layers.Concatenate(name="sample_conditional_router_input")([eye_repr, head_repr, hand_repr])
  router = layers.Dense(128, activation="relu", name="sample_router_hidden")(router_input)
  router = layers.Dense(3, activation="softmax", name="sample_conditional_router")(router)
  fused = layers.Dot(axes=(1, 1), name="emoe_weighted_experts")([router, expert_stack])
  main_probs = classifier_from_repr(fused, "emoe_classifier")
  outputs = duplicate_outputs(main_probs, include_hard=True)
  name="rq2_emoe_unimodal_distillation",
  emoe_preview = build_emoe_unimodal_distillation_model()
  emoe_preview.summary()

CELL 29
  def make_participant_exclusive_split(record_df, domain_name, n_splits=10, random_state=SEED):
  """Return train/val/test DataFrames for one domain only."""
  record_df = record_df.reset_index(drop=True).copy()
  sgkf = StratifiedGroupKFold(n_splits=n_splits, shuffle=True, random_state=random_state)
  sgkf.split(record_df["path"], record_df["Yemo"], groups=record_df["participant"])
  train_df = record_df[record_df["fold"].between(0, 6)].reset_index(drop=True)
  val_df = record_df[record_df["fold"].between(7, 8)].reset_index(drop=True)
  test_df = record_df[record_df["fold"] == 9].reset_index(drop=True)
  print(f"\n[{domain_name}] participant-exclusive split")
  print("Train participants:", sorted(train_df["participant"].unique()))
  print("Val participants:", sorted(val_df["participant"].unique()))
  print("Test participants:", sorted(test_df["participant"].unique()))
  print("Train participant count:", train_df["participant"].nunique())
  print("Val participant count:", val_df["participant"].nunique())
  print("Test participant count:", test_df["participant"].nunique())
  assert set(train_df["participant"]).isdisjoint(val_df["participant"])
  assert set(train_df["participant"]).isdisjoint(test_df["participant"])
  assert set(val_df["participant"]).isdisjoint(test_df["participant"])
  print("Train distribution:")
  display(pd.crosstab(train_df["participant"], train_df["emotion"]))
  print("Validation distribution:")
  display(pd.crosstab(val_df["participant"], val_df["emotion"]))
  print("Test distribution:")
  display(pd.crosstab(test_df["participant"], test_df["emotion"]))
  return {"all": record_df, "train": train_df, "val": val_df, "test": test_df}

  test_records = record_splits[ACTIVE_DOMAIN]["test"]
CELL 31
  def downsample_60hz_to_10hz(df, feature_cols):
  df = df[feature_cols].apply(pd.to_numeric, errors="coerce")
  df = df.replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan)
  df = df.ffill().bfill().fillna(0.0)
  usable_rows = (len(df) // DOWNSAMPLE_FACTOR) * DOWNSAMPLE_FACTOR
  df = df.iloc[:usable_rows]
  df_10hz = (
  df.groupby(np.arange(len(df)) // DOWNSAMPLE_FACTOR)
  recording_mean = df_10hz.mean()
  df_10hz = (df_10hz - recording_mean)
  df_10hz = df_10hz / (df_10hz.max() + 1e-8)
  return df_10hz

  def run_pca_for_modality(X_modality, feature_cols, modality_name):

CELL 71
  # ---- TCN expert blocks ----
  def _tcn_block(x, filters, kernel_size, dilation_rate, dropout=0.2, name=None):
  res = x
  y = layers.Conv1D(filters, kernel_size, padding="causal",
  dilation_rate=dilation_rate, name=None if name is None else name+"_c1")(x)
  y = layers.BatchNormalization(name=None if name is None else name+"_bn1")(y)
  y = layers.ReLU(name=None if name is None else name+"_relu1")(y)
  y = layers.Dropout(dropout, name=None if name is None else name+"_do1")(y)
  y = layers.Conv1D(filters, kernel_size, padding="causal",
  dilation_rate=dilation_rate, name=None if name is None else name+"_c2")(y)
  y = layers.BatchNormalization(name=None if name is None else name+"_bn2")(y)
  res = layers.Conv1D(filters, 1, padding="same", name=None if name is None else name+"_proj")(res)
  res = layers.BatchNormalization(name=None if name is None else name+"_proj_bn")(res)
  y = layers.Add(name=None if name is None else name+"_add")([y, res])
  y = layers.ReLU(name=None if name is None else name+"_relu2")(y)
  def build_expert_tcn(x, name_prefix="exp"):
  y = _tcn_block(x, 128, 3, 1, 0.2, name=name_prefix+"_tcn1")
  y = _tcn_block(y, 128, 3, 2, 0.2, name=name_prefix+"_tcn2")
  y = _tcn_block(y, 128, 3, 4, 0.2, name=name_prefix+"_tcn3")
  y = layers.GlobalAveragePooling1D(name=name_prefix+"_gap")(y)
  class Top2Gate(layers.Layer):
  def __init__(self, num_experts=3, gate_hidden=128, tau_init=2.0,
  lb_coef=0.02, ent_coef=0.01, name="gate", **kwargs):
  self.num_experts = num_experts
  self.gate_hidden = gate_hidden
  self.tau = tf.Variable(tau_init, dtype=tf.float32, trainable=False, name=f"{name}_tau")
  self.d1 = layers.Dense(gate_hidden, activation="relu", name=f"{name}_dense1")
  self.d2 = layers.Dense(num_experts, activation=None, name=f"{name}_logits")
  def call(self, gate_in, training=None):
  logits = self.d2(self.d1(gate_in))
  w_soft = tf.nn.softmax(logits / self.tau, -1)
  idx = top2.indices
  vals = tf.nn.softmax(top2.values, -1)
  B = tf.shape(w_soft)[0]
  row_ids = tf.repeat(tf.range(B), repeats=2)
  col_ids = tf.reshape(idx, (-1,))
  scatter_idx = tf.stack([row_ids, col_ids], axis=1)
  scatter_vals = tf.reshape(vals, (-1,))
  w_sparse = tf.scatter_nd(scatter_idx, scatter_vals, (B, self.num_experts))
  mean_usage = tf.reduce_mean(w_sparse, axis=0)
  uniform = tf.fill([self.num_experts], 1.0 / tf.cast(self.num_experts, tf.float32))
  lb_loss = tf.reduce_sum(tf.square(mean_usage - uniform))
  ent = -tf.reduce_mean(tf.reduce_sum(w_soft * tf.math.log(tf.clip_by_value(w_soft, 1e-8, 1.0)), axis=-1))
  class GateTemperatureScheduler(callbacks.Callback):
  def __init__(self, gate_layer, start_tau=2.0, end_tau=0.7, anneal_epochs=20):
  self.gate = gate_layer
  self.anneal_epochs = max(1, int(anneal_epochs))
  def on_epoch_begin(self, epoch, logs=None):
  p = min(1.0, epoch / self.anneal_epochs)
  tau = self.start_tau + p * (self.end_tau - self.start_tau)
  self.gate.tau.assign(tau)
  K.set_value(self.gate.tau, tau)
  if epoch % 5 == 0:
  print(f"[GateTemperatureScheduler] epoch={epoch} tau={tau:.3f}")
  # ---- Same teacher single-input MoE builder, adapted only by model name ----
  def build_moe_single_input(
  tau_init=2.0, tau_final=0.7,
  gate_hidden=128, classifier_hidden=256,
  lb_coef=0.02, ent_coef=0.01,
  name="Student_MoE_SingleInput_3experts_Top2Gate"
  seq_in = layers.Input(shape=input_shape, name="seq")
  exp_nat = build_expert_tcn(seq_in, "exp_nat")
  exp_act = build_expert_tcn(seq_in, "exp_act")
  exp_shared = build_expert_tcn(seq_in, "exp_shared")
  E_stack = layers.Concatenate(axis=1, name="experts_stack")([e1, e2, e3])
  gate_in = layers.GlobalAveragePooling1D(name="gate_pool")(seq_in)
  gate_layer = Top2Gate(num_experts=3, gate_hidden=gate_hidden,
  tau_init=tau_init, lb_coef=lb_coef, ent_coef=ent_coef, name="gate")
  w_sparse, w_soft = gate_layer(gate_in)
  w_expanded = layers.Lambda(lambda z: tf.expand_dims(z, -1), name="w_expand")(w_sparse)

  def downsample_60hz_to_10hz_numeric(df, feature_cols):
  missing = [col for col in feature_cols if col not in df.columns]
  raise ValueError(f"Missing required columns: {missing[:10]} total={len(missing)}")
  features = df[feature_cols].apply(pd.to_numeric, errors="coerce")
  features = features.replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan)
  features = features.ffill().bfill().fillna(0.0)
  usable_rows = (len(features) // DOWNSAMPLE_FACTOR) * DOWNSAMPLE_FACTOR
  features = features.iloc[:usable_rows]
  .groupby(np.arange(len(features)) // DOWNSAMPLE_FACTOR)
  def make_aligned_student_teacher_windows(row):
  raw = pd.read_csv(row["path"])
  student_df = downsample_60hz_to_10hz(raw, STUDENT_COLS)
  teacher_df = downsample_60hz_to_10hz_numeric(raw, AU_NAMES)
  usable = min(len(student_df), len(teacher_df))
  student_df = student_df.iloc[:usable].reset_index(drop=True)
  teacher_df = teacher_df.iloc[:usable].reset_index(drop=True)
  starts = teacher_style_keep_starts_from_au(
  teacher_df,
  seq_length=SEQ_LENGTH,
  X_eye, X_head, X_hand, X_teacher, Yemo, Ydom = [], [], [], [], [], []
  student_window = student_df.iloc[start:start + SEQ_LENGTH]
  teacher_window = teacher_df.iloc[start:start + SEQ_LENGTH]
  if len(student_window) != SEQ_LENGTH or len(teacher_window) != SEQ_LENGTH:
  X_eye.append(student_window[EYE_COLS].to_numpy(dtype=np.float32))
  X_head.append(student_window[HEAD_COLS].to_numpy(dtype=np.float32))
  X_hand.append(student_window[HAND_COLS].to_numpy(dtype=np.float32))
  X_teacher.append(teacher_window[AU_NAMES].to_numpy(dtype=np.float32))
  return X_eye, X_head, X_hand, X_teacher, Yemo, Ydom
  def load_aligned_split(record_df):
  X_eye, X_head, X_hand, X_teacher, Yemo, Ydom = [], [], [], [], [], []
  eye, head, hand, teacher, emo, dom = make_aligned_student_teacher_windows(row)
  X_teacher.extend(teacher)

CELL 113
  def soften_probabilities(probabilities, temperature=1.0):
  probabilities = np.asarray(probabilities, dtype=np.float32)
  probabilities = np.clip(probabilities, 1e-8, 1.0)
  if temperature == 1.0:
  softened = probabilities
  logits = np.log(probabilities) / float(temperature)
  logits = logits - logits.max(axis=1, keepdims=True)
  softened = np.exp(logits)
  softened = softened / softened.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)
  class Top2Gate(layers.Layer):
  def __init__(self, num_experts=3, gate_hidden=128, tau_init=2.0,
  lb_coef=0.02, ent_coef=0.01, name="gate", **kwargs):
  self.num_experts = num_experts
  self.gate_hidden = gate_hidden
  self.tau = tf.Variable(tau_init, dtype=tf.float32, trainable=False, name=f"{name}_tau")
  self.d1 = layers.Dense(gate_hidden, activation="relu", name=f"{name}_dense1")
  self.d2 = layers.Dense(num_experts, activation=None, name=f"{name}_logits")
  def call(self, gate_in, training=None):
  logits = self.d2(self.d1(gate_in))
  w_soft = tf.nn.softmax(logits / self.tau, axis=-1)
  idx = top2.indices
  vals = tf.nn.softmax(top2.values, axis=-1)
  batch_size = tf.shape(w_soft)[0]
  row_ids = tf.repeat(tf.range(batch_size), repeats=2)
  col_ids = tf.reshape(idx, (-1,))
  scatter_idx = tf.stack([row_ids, col_ids], axis=1)
  scatter_vals = tf.reshape(vals, (-1,))
  w_sparse = tf.scatter_nd(scatter_idx, scatter_vals, (batch_size, self.num_experts))
  mean_usage = tf.reduce_mean(w_sparse, axis=0)
  uniform = tf.fill([self.num_experts], 1.0 / tf.cast(self.num_experts, tf.float32))
  lb_loss = tf.reduce_sum(tf.square(mean_usage - uniform))
  ent = -tf.reduce_mean(
  tf.reduce_sum(w_soft * tf.math.log(tf.clip_by_value(w_soft, 1e-8, 1.0)), axis=-1)
  def get_config(self):
  config = super().get_config()
  "num_experts": self.num_experts,
  "gate_hidden": self.gate_hidden,
  def _teacher_tcn_block(x, filters, kernel_size, dilation_rate, dropout=0.2, name=None):
  res = x
  y = layers.Conv1D(
  padding="causal",
  dilation_rate=dilation_rate,
  name=None if name is None else name + "_c1",
  y = layers.BatchNormalization(name=None if name is None else name + "_bn1")(y)
  y = layers.ReLU(name=None if name is None else name + "_relu1")(y)
  y = layers.Dropout(dropout, name=None if name is None else name + "_do1")(y)
  y = layers.Conv1D(
  padding="causal",
  dilation_rate=dilation_rate,
  name=None if name is None else name + "_c2",
  y = layers.BatchNormalization(name=None if name is None else name + "_bn2")(y)
  res = layers.Conv1D(filters, 1, padding="same", name=None if name is None else name + "_proj")(res)
  res = layers.BatchNormalization(name=None if name is None else name + "_proj_bn")(res)
  y = layers.Add(name=None if name is None else name + "_add")([y, res])
  def _teacher_expert_tcn(x, name_prefix):
  y = _teacher_tcn_block(x, 128, 3, 1, 0.2, name=name_prefix + "_tcn1")
  y = _teacher_tcn_block(y, 128, 3, 2, 0.2, name=name_prefix + "_tcn2")
  y = _teacher_tcn_block(y, 128, 3, 4, 0.2, name=name_prefix + "_tcn3")
  def build_teacher_moe_fallback():
  seq_in = layers.Input(shape=(SEQ_LENGTH, len(AU_NAMES)), name="seq")
  exp_nat = _teacher_expert_tcn(seq_in, "exp_nat")
  exp_act = _teacher_expert_tcn(seq_in, "exp_act")
  exp_shared = _teacher_expert_tcn(seq_in, "exp_shared")
  expert_stack = layers.Concatenate(axis=1, name="experts_stack")([e1, e2, e3])
  gate_in = layers.GlobalAveragePooling1D(name="gate_pool")(seq_in)
  gate_layer = Top2Gate(
  num_experts=3,
  gate_hidden=128,
  tau_init=2.0,
  lb_coef=0.02,

  def infer_best_teacher_column_mapping(y_soft, y_true):
  pred = np.argmax(y_soft, axis=1)
  num_classes = len(le.classes_)
  confusion = confusion_matrix(y_true, pred, labels=np.arange(num_classes))
  best_perm = None
  best_correct = -1
  correct = sum(confusion[perm[col], col] for col in range(num_classes))
  best_correct = correct
  best_perm = perm
  mapping = {col: best_perm[col] for col in range(num_classes)}
  mapped_pred = np.array([mapping[col] for col in pred])
  def apply_teacher_column_mapping(y_soft, mapping):
  remapped = np.zeros_like(y_soft)
  for teacher_col, hard_label_col in mapping.items():
  remapped[:, hard_label_col] = y_soft[:, teacher_col]

  def tcn_expert(input_tensor, name, units=128, dropout=0.2):
  x = layers.Conv1D(units, 3, padding="causal", dilation_rate=1, activation="relu", name=f"{name}_conv1")(input_tensor)
  x = layers.BatchNormalization(name=f"{name}_bn1")(x)
  x = layers.Dropout(dropout, name=f"{name}_drop1")(x)
  x = layers.Conv1D(units, 3, padding="causal", dilation_rate=2, activation="relu", name=f"{name}_conv2")(x)
  x = layers.BatchNormalization(name=f"{name}_bn2")(x)
  x = layers.Conv1D(units, 3, padding="causal", dilation_rate=4, activation="relu", name=f"{name}_conv3")(x)
  x = layers.GlobalAveragePooling1D(name=f"{name}_pool")(x)
  return layers.Dense(units, activation="relu", name=f"{name}_repr")(x)
  def classifier_from_repr(repr_tensor, name, hidden=256, dropout=0.3):
  x = layers.Dense(hidden, activation="relu", name=f"{name}_hidden")(repr_tensor)
  x = layers.Dropout(dropout, name=f"{name}_dropout")(x)
  return layers.Dense(len(le.classes_), activation="softmax", name=f"{name}_probs")(x)
  def duplicate_outputs(main_probs, include_hard=True, prefix=""):
  outputs = {"soft_output": layers.Lambda(lambda z: z, name=f"{prefix}soft_output")(main_probs)}
  def callbacks_for(name, monitor="val_soft_output_accuracy", checkpoint_dir=None):

  monitor_mode = "max" if "accuracy" in monitor or "f1" in monitor else "min"
  monitor=monitor,
  mode=monitor_mode,
  patience=8,
  restore_best_weights=True,
  monitor=monitor,
  mode=monitor_mode,
  save_best_only=True,
  def json_ready(value):
  if isinstance(value, dict):
  return {str(k): json_ready(v) for k, v in value.items()}
  if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
  return [json_ready(v) for v in value]
  if isinstance(value, np.ndarray):
  return value.tolist()
  if isinstance(value, (np.integer,)):
  return int(value)
  if isinstance(value, (np.floating,)):
  return float(value)
  if isinstance(value, (np.bool_,)):
  return bool(value)
  if isinstance(value, Path):
  return str(value)
  return value

  def build_coarse_domain_moe_model():

  layers.Reshape((1, 128), name="natural_expert_axis")(natural_expert),
  layers.Reshape((1, 128), name="acted_expert_axis")(acted_expert),

  fused = layers.Dot(axes=(1, 1), name="domain_weighted_experts")([gate, expert_stack])
  probs = classifier_from_repr(fused, "coarse_moe_classifier")
  name="rq2_coarse_domain_moe",
  coarse_preview = build_coarse_domain_moe_model()

  def build_emoe_unimodal_distillation_model():
  eye_input = layers.Input(shape=(SEQ_LENGTH, len(EYE_COLS)), name="eye_input")
  head_input = layers.Input(shape=(SEQ_LENGTH, len(HEAD_COLS)), name="head_input")
  hand_input = layers.Input(shape=(SEQ_LENGTH, len(HAND_COLS)), name="hand_input")
  eye_repr = tcn_expert(eye_input, "eye_expert")
  head_repr = tcn_expert(head_input, "head_expert")
  hand_repr = tcn_expert(hand_input, "hand_expert")
  expert_stack = layers.Concatenate(axis=1, name="modality_expert_stack")([
  layers.Reshape((1, 128), name="eye_expert_axis")(eye_repr),
  layers.Reshape((1, 128), name="head_expert_axis")(head_repr),
  layers.Reshape((1, 128), name="hand_expert_axis")(hand_repr),
  router_input = layers.Concatenate(name="sample_conditional_router_input")([eye_repr, head_repr, hand_repr])
  router = layers.Dense(128, activation="relu", name="sample_router_hidden")(router_input)
  router = layers.Dense(3, activation="softmax", name="sample_conditional_router")(router)
  fused = layers.Dot(axes=(1, 1), name="emoe_weighted_experts")([router, expert_stack])
  main_probs = classifier_from_repr(fused, "emoe_classifier")
  outputs = duplicate_outputs(main_probs, include_hard=True)
  name="rq2_emoe_unimodal_distillation",
  emoe_preview = build_emoe_unimodal_distillation_model()
  emoe_preview.summary()

  def json_ready(value):
  if isinstance(value, dict):
  return {str(k): json_ready(v) for k, v in value.items()}
  if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
  return [json_ready(v) for v in value]
  if isinstance(value, np.ndarray):
  return value.tolist()
  if isinstance(value, (np.integer,)):
  return int(value)
  if isinstance(value, (np.floating,)):
  return float(value)
  if isinstance(value, (np.bool_,)):
  return bool(value)
  if isinstance(value, Path):
  return str(value)
  return value

CELL 29
  def make_participant_exclusive_split(record_df, domain_name, n_splits=10, random_state=SEED):
  """Return train/val/test DataFrames for one domain only."""
  record_df = record_df.reset_index(drop=True).copy()
  sgkf = StratifiedGroupKFold(n_splits=n_splits, shuffle=True, random_state=random_state)
  sgkf.split(record_df["path"], record_df["Yemo"], groups=record_df["participant"])
  train_df = record_df[record_df["fold"].between(0, 6)].reset_index(drop=True)
  val_df = record_df[record_df["fold"].between(7, 8)].reset_index(drop=True)
  test_df = record_df[record_df["fold"] == 9].reset_index(drop=True)
  print(f"\n[{domain_name}] participant-exclusive split")
  print("Train participants:", sorted(train_df["participant"].unique()))
  print("Val participants:", sorted(val_df["participant"].unique()))
  print("Test participants:", sorted(test_df["participant"].unique()))
  print("Train participant count:", train_df["participant"].nunique())
  print("Val participant count:", val_df["participant"].nunique())
  print("Test participant count:", test_df["participant"].nunique())
  assert set(train_df["participant"]).isdisjoint(val_df["participant"])
  assert set(train_df["participant"]).isdisjoint(test_df["participant"])
  assert set(val_df["participant"]).isdisjoint(test_df["participant"])
  print("Train distribution:")
  display(pd.crosstab(train_df["participant"], train_df["emotion"]))
  print("Validation distribution:")
  display(pd.crosstab(val_df["participant"], val_df["emotion"]))
  print("Test distribution:")
  display(pd.crosstab(test_df["participant"], test_df["emotion"]))
  return {"all": record_df, "train": train_df, "val": val_df, "test": test_df}

CELL 31
  def downsample_60hz_to_10hz(df, feature_cols):
  df = df[feature_cols].apply(pd.to_numeric, errors="coerce")
  df = df.replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan)
  df = df.ffill().bfill().fillna(0.0)
  usable_rows = (len(df) // DOWNSAMPLE_FACTOR) * DOWNSAMPLE_FACTOR
  df = df.iloc[:usable_rows]
  df_10hz = (
  df.groupby(np.arange(len(df)) // DOWNSAMPLE_FACTOR)
  recording_mean = df_10hz.mean()
  df_10hz = (df_10hz - recording_mean)
  df_10hz = df_10hz / (df_10hz.max() + 1e-8)
  return df_10hz

  def run_pca_for_modality(X_modality, feature_cols, modality_name):
  # Average each 5-frame window into one value per signal
  X_mean = X_modality.mean(axis=1)
  # Standardize before PCA
  X_scaled = StandardScaler().fit_transform(X_mean)
  pca = PCA()
  X_pca = pca.fit_transform(X_scaled)
  explained = pca.explained_variance_ratio_
  cumulative = np.cumsum(explained)

  loadings_df = pd.DataFrame(
  index=feature_cols,
  columns=[f"PC{i+1}" for i in range(len(feature_cols))]

CELL 72
  # ---- TCN expert blocks ----
  def _tcn_block(x, filters, kernel_size, dilation_rate, dropout=0.2, name=None):
  res = x
  y = layers.Conv1D(filters, kernel_size, padding="causal",
  dilation_rate=dilation_rate, name=None if name is None else name+"_c1")(x)
  y = layers.BatchNormalization(name=None if name is None else name+"_bn1")(y)
  y = layers.ReLU(name=None if name is None else name+"_relu1")(y)
  y = layers.Dropout(dropout, name=None if name is None else name+"_do1")(y)
  y = layers.Conv1D(filters, kernel_size, padding="causal",
  dilation_rate=dilation_rate, name=None if name is None else name+"_c2")(y)
  y = layers.BatchNormalization(name=None if name is None else name+"_bn2")(y)
  res = layers.Conv1D(filters, 1, padding="same", name=None if name is None else name+"_proj")(res)
  res = layers.BatchNormalization(name=None if name is None else name+"_proj_bn")(res)
  y = layers.Add(name=None if name is None else name+"_add")([y, res])
  y = layers.ReLU(name=None if name is None else name+"_relu2")(y)
  def build_expert_tcn(x, name_prefix="exp"):
  y = _tcn_block(x, 128, 3, 1, 0.2, name=name_prefix+"_tcn1")
  y = _tcn_block(y, 128, 3, 2, 0.2, name=name_prefix+"_tcn2")
  y = _tcn_block(y, 128, 3, 4, 0.2, name=name_prefix+"_tcn3")
  y = layers.GlobalAveragePooling1D(name=name_prefix+"_gap")(y)
  class Top2Gate(layers.Layer):
  def __init__(self, num_experts=3, gate_hidden=128, tau_init=2.0,
  lb_coef=0.02, ent_coef=0.01, name="gate", **kwargs):
  self.num_experts = num_experts
  self.gate_hidden = gate_hidden
  self.tau = tf.Variable(tau_init, dtype=tf.float32, trainable=False, name=f"{name}_tau")
  self.d1 = layers.Dense(gate_hidden, activation="relu", name=f"{name}_dense1")
  self.d2 = layers.Dense(num_experts, activation=None, name=f"{name}_logits")
  def call(self, gate_in, training=None):
  logits = self.d2(self.d1(gate_in))
  w_soft = tf.nn.softmax(logits / self.tau, -1)
  idx = top2.indices
  vals = tf.nn.softmax(top2.values, -1)
  B = tf.shape(w_soft)[0]
  row_ids = tf.repeat(tf.range(B), repeats=2)
  col_ids = tf.reshape(idx, (-1,))
  scatter_idx = tf.stack([row_ids, col_ids], axis=1)
  scatter_vals = tf.reshape(vals, (-1,))
  w_sparse = tf.scatter_nd(scatter_idx, scatter_vals, (B, self.num_experts))
  mean_usage = tf.reduce_mean(w_sparse, axis=0)
  uniform = tf.fill([self.num_experts], 1.0 / tf.cast(self.num_experts, tf.float32))
  lb_loss = tf.reduce_sum(tf.square(mean_usage - uniform))
  ent = -tf.reduce_mean(tf.reduce_sum(w_soft * tf.math.log(tf.clip_by_value(w_soft, 1e-8, 1.0)), axis=-1))
  class GateTemperatureScheduler(callbacks.Callback):
  def __init__(self, gate_layer, start_tau=2.0, end_tau=0.7, anneal_epochs=20):
  self.gate = gate_layer
  self.anneal_epochs = max(1, int(anneal_epochs))
  def on_epoch_begin(self, epoch, logs=None):
  p = min(1.0, epoch / self.anneal_epochs)
  tau = self.start_tau + p * (self.end_tau - self.start_tau)
  self.gate.tau.assign(tau)
  K.set_value(self.gate.tau, tau)
  if epoch % 5 == 0:
  print(f"[GateTemperatureScheduler] epoch={epoch} tau={tau:.3f}")
  # ---- Same teacher single-input MoE builder, adapted only by model name ----
  def build_moe_single_input(
  tau_init=2.0, tau_final=0.7,
  gate_hidden=128, classifier_hidden=256,
  lb_coef=0.02, ent_coef=0.01,
  name="Student_MoE_SingleInput_3experts_Top2Gate"
  seq_in = layers.Input(shape=input_shape, name="seq")
  exp_nat = build_expert_tcn(seq_in, "exp_nat")
  exp_act = build_expert_tcn(seq_in, "exp_act")
  exp_shared = build_expert_tcn(seq_in, "exp_shared")
  E_stack = layers.Concatenate(axis=1, name="experts_stack")([e1, e2, e3])
  gate_in = layers.GlobalAveragePooling1D(name="gate_pool")(seq_in)
  gate_layer = Top2Gate(num_experts=3, gate_hidden=gate_hidden,
  tau_init=tau_init, lb_coef=lb_coef, ent_coef=ent_coef, name="gate")
  w_sparse, w_soft = gate_layer(gate_in)
  w_expanded = layers.Lambda(lambda z: tf.expand_dims(z, -1), name="w_expand")(w_sparse)

  def downsample_60hz_to_10hz_numeric(df, feature_cols):
  missing = [col for col in feature_cols if col not in df.columns]
  raise ValueError(f"Missing required columns: {missing[:10]} total={len(missing)}")
  features = df[feature_cols].apply(pd.to_numeric, errors="coerce")
  features = features.replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan)
  features = features.ffill().bfill().fillna(0.0)
  usable_rows = (len(features) // DOWNSAMPLE_FACTOR) * DOWNSAMPLE_FACTOR
  features = features.iloc[:usable_rows]
  .groupby(np.arange(len(features)) // DOWNSAMPLE_FACTOR)

  def soften_probabilities(probabilities, temperature=1.0):
  probabilities = np.asarray(probabilities, dtype=np.float32)
  probabilities = np.clip(probabilities, 1e-8, 1.0)
  if temperature == 1.0:
  softened = probabilities
  logits = np.log(probabilities) / float(temperature)
  logits = logits - logits.max(axis=1, keepdims=True)
  softened = np.exp(logits)
  softened = softened / softened.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)
  class Top2Gate(layers.Layer):
  def __init__(self, num_experts=3, gate_hidden=128, tau_init=2.0,
  lb_coef=0.02, ent_coef=0.01, name="gate", **kwargs):
  self.num_experts = num_experts
  self.gate_hidden = gate_hidden
  self.tau = tf.Variable(tau_init, dtype=tf.float32, trainable=False, name=f"{name}_tau")
  self.d1 = layers.Dense(gate_hidden, activation="relu", name=f"{name}_dense1")
  self.d2 = layers.Dense(num_experts, activation=None, name=f"{name}_logits")
  def call(self, gate_in, training=None):
  logits = self.d2(self.d1(gate_in))
  w_soft = tf.nn.softmax(logits / self.tau, axis=-1)
  idx = top2.indices
  vals = tf.nn.softmax(top2.values, axis=-1)
  batch_size = tf.shape(w_soft)[0]
  row_ids = tf.repeat(tf.range(batch_size), repeats=2)
  col_ids = tf.reshape(idx, (-1,))
  scatter_idx = tf.stack([row_ids, col_ids], axis=1)
  scatter_vals = tf.reshape(vals, (-1,))
  w_sparse = tf.scatter_nd(scatter_idx, scatter_vals, (batch_size, self.num_experts))
  mean_usage = tf.reduce_mean(w_sparse, axis=0)
  uniform = tf.fill([self.num_experts], 1.0 / tf.cast(self.num_experts, tf.float32))
  lb_loss = tf.reduce_sum(tf.square(mean_usage - uniform))
  ent = -tf.reduce_mean(
  tf.reduce_sum(w_soft * tf.math.log(tf.clip_by_value(w_soft, 1e-8, 1.0)), axis=-1)
  def get_config(self):
  config = super().get_config()
  "num_experts": self.num_experts,
  "gate_hidden": self.gate_hidden,
  def _teacher_tcn_block(x, filters, kernel_size, dilation_rate, dropout=0.2, name=None):
  res = x
  y = layers.Conv1D(
  padding="causal",
  dilation_rate=dilation_rate,
  name=None if name is None else name + "_c1",
  y = layers.BatchNormalization(name=None if name is None else name + "_bn1")(y)
  y = layers.ReLU(name=None if name is None else name + "_relu1")(y)
  y = layers.Dropout(dropout, name=None if name is None else name + "_do1")(y)
  y = layers.Conv1D(
  padding="causal",
  dilation_rate=dilation_rate,
  name=None if name is None else name + "_c2",
  y = layers.BatchNormalization(name=None if name is None else name + "_bn2")(y)
  res = layers.Conv1D(filters, 1, padding="same", name=None if name is None else name + "_proj")(res)
  res = layers.BatchNormalization(name=None if name is None else name + "_proj_bn")(res)
  y = layers.Add(name=None if name is None else name + "_add")([y, res])
  def _teacher_expert_tcn(x, name_prefix):
  y = _teacher_tcn_block(x, 128, 3, 1, 0.2, name=name_prefix + "_tcn1")
  y = _teacher_tcn_block(y, 128, 3, 2, 0.2, name=name_prefix + "_tcn2")
  y = _teacher_tcn_block(y, 128, 3, 4, 0.2, name=name_prefix + "_tcn3")
  def build_teacher_moe_fallback():
  seq_in = layers.Input(shape=(SEQ_LENGTH, len(AU_NAMES)), name="seq")
  exp_nat = _teacher_expert_tcn(seq_in, "exp_nat")
  exp_act = _teacher_expert_tcn(seq_in, "exp_act")
  exp_shared = _teacher_expert_tcn(seq_in, "exp_shared")
  expert_stack = layers.Concatenate(axis=1, name="experts_stack")([e1, e2, e3])
  gate_in = layers.GlobalAveragePooling1D(name="gate_pool")(seq_in)
  gate_layer = Top2Gate(
  num_experts=3,
  gate_hidden=128,
  tau_init=2.0,
  lb_coef=0.02,

  def infer_best_teacher_column_mapping(y_soft, y_true):
  pred = np.argmax(y_soft, axis=1)
  num_classes = len(le.classes_)
  confusion = confusion_matrix(y_true, pred, labels=np.arange(num_classes))
  best_perm = None
  best_correct = -1
  correct = sum(confusion[perm[col], col] for col in range(num_classes))
  best_correct = correct
  best_perm = perm
  mapping = {col: best_perm[col] for col in range(num_classes)}
  mapped_pred = np.array([mapping[col] for col in pred])
  def apply_teacher_column_mapping(y_soft, mapping):

  remapped[:, hard_label_col] = y_soft[:, teacher_col]

  def tcn_expert(input_tensor, name, units=128, dropout=0.2):
  x = layers.Conv1D(units, 3, padding="causal", dilation_rate=1, activation="relu", name=f"{name}_conv1")(input_tensor)
  x = layers.BatchNormalization(name=f"{name}_bn1")(x)
  x = layers.Dropout(dropout, name=f"{name}_drop1")(x)
  x = layers.Conv1D(units, 3, padding="causal", dilation_rate=2, activation="relu", name=f"{name}_conv2")(x)
  x = layers.BatchNormalization(name=f"{name}_bn2")(x)
  x = layers.Conv1D(units, 3, padding="causal", dilation_rate=4, activation="relu", name=f"{name}_conv3")(x)
  x = layers.GlobalAveragePooling1D(name=f"{name}_pool")(x)
  return layers.Dense(units, activation="relu", name=f"{name}_repr")(x)
  def classifier_from_repr(repr_tensor, name, hidden=256, dropout=0.3):
  x = layers.Dense(hidden, activation="relu", name=f"{name}_hidden")(repr_tensor)
  x = layers.Dropout(dropout, name=f"{name}_dropout")(x)
  return layers.Dense(len(le.classes_), activation="softmax", name=f"{name}_probs")(x)
  def duplicate_outputs(main_probs, include_hard=True, prefix=""):
  outputs = {"soft_output": layers.Lambda(lambda z: z, name=f"{prefix}soft_output")(main_probs)}

  monitor_mode = "max" if "accuracy" in monitor or "f1" in monitor else "min"
  monitor=monitor,
  mode=monitor_mode,
  patience=8,
  restore_best_weights=True,
  monitor=monitor,
  mode=monitor_mode,
  save_best_only=True,
  def json_ready(value):
  if isinstance(value, dict):
  return {str(k): json_ready(v) for k, v in value.items()}
  if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
  return [json_ready(v) for v in value]
  if isinstance(value, np.ndarray):
  return value.tolist()
  if isinstance(value, (np.integer,)):
  return int(value)
  if isinstance(value, (np.floating,)):
  return float(value)
  if isinstance(value, (np.bool_,)):
  return bool(value)
  if isinstance(value, Path):
  return str(value)
  return value

  def build_coarse_domain_moe_model():
  flat_input = layers.Input(shape=(SEQ_LENGTH, len(STUDENT_COLS)), name="flat_student_input")
  domain_input = layers.Input(shape=(), dtype=tf.int32, name="domain_input")
  natural_expert = tcn_expert(flat_input, "natural_domain_expert")
  acted_expert = tcn_expert(flat_input, "acted_domain_expert")
  shared_expert = tcn_expert(flat_input, "shared_domain_expert")
  expert_stack = layers.Concatenate(axis=1, name="domain_expert_stack")([
  layers.Reshape((1, 128), name="natural_expert_axis")(natural_expert),
  layers.Reshape((1, 128), name="acted_expert_axis")(acted_expert),
  layers.Reshape((1, 128), name="shared_expert_axis")(shared_expert),
  domain_one_hot = layers.Lambda(
  name="domain_one_hot",
  gate = layers.Dense(16, activation="relu", name="domain_gate_hidden")(domain_one_hot)
  gate = layers.Dense(3, activation="softmax", name="domain_gate")(gate)
  fused = layers.Dot(axes=(1, 1), name="domain_weighted_experts")([gate, expert_stack])
  probs = classifier_from_repr(fused, "coarse_moe_classifier")
  name="rq2_coarse_domain_moe",
  coarse_preview = build_coarse_domain_moe_model()

  def build_emoe_model(expert_units=RQ2_EMOE_EXPERT_UNITS, classifier_hidden=RQ2_EMOE_CLASSIFIER_HIDDEN):
  eye_input  = layers.Input(shape=(SEQ_LENGTH, len(EYE_COLS)),  name="eye_input")
  head_input = layers.Input(shape=(SEQ_LENGTH, len(HEAD_COLS)), name="head_input")
  hand_input = layers.Input(shape=(SEQ_LENGTH, len(HAND_COLS)), name="hand_input")
  # Modality-specific experts
  eye_repr  = tcn_expert(eye_input,  "eye_expert",  units=expert_units)
  head_repr = tcn_expert(head_input, "head_expert", units=expert_units)
  hand_repr = tcn_expert(hand_input, "hand_expert", units=expert_units)
  # Unimodal distillation heads — each expert predicts on its own
  eye_unimodal  = classifier_from_repr(eye_repr,  "eye_unimodal_classifier",  hidden=classifier_hidden)
  head_unimodal = classifier_from_repr(head_repr, "head_unimodal_classifier", hidden=classifier_hidden)
  hand_unimodal = classifier_from_repr(hand_repr, "hand_unimodal_classifier", hidden=classifier_hidden)
  # Sample-conditional gate — learned from all three representations
  gate_in = layers.Concatenate(name="gate_concat")([eye_repr, head_repr, hand_repr])
  gate = layers.Dense(64, activation="relu", name="gate_hidden")(gate_in)
  gate = layers.Dense(3, activation="softmax", name="gate_weights")(gate)
  # Stack experts and fuse via gate weights
  expert_stack = layers.Concatenate(axis=1, name="expert_stack")([
  layers.Reshape((1, expert_units), name="eye_axis")(eye_repr),
  layers.Reshape((1, expert_units), name="head_axis")(head_repr),
  layers.Reshape((1, expert_units), name="hand_axis")(hand_repr),
  fused = layers.Dot(axes=(1, 1), name="gated_fusion")([gate, expert_stack])
  probs = classifier_from_repr(fused, "emoe_classifier", hidden=classifier_hidden)
  outputs = {
  name="rq2_emoe_unimodal_distillation",
  emoe_preview = build_emoe_model()
  emoe_preview.summary()
CELL 150

  def downsample_60hz_to_10hz(df, feature_cols):
  df = df[feature_cols].apply(pd.to_numeric, errors="coerce")
  df = df.replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan)
  df = df.ffill().bfill().fillna(0.0)
  usable_rows = (len(df) // DOWNSAMPLE_FACTOR) * DOWNSAMPLE_FACTOR
  df = df.iloc[:usable_rows]
  df_10hz = (
  df.groupby(np.arange(len(df)) // DOWNSAMPLE_FACTOR)
  return df_10hz
CELL 19
  def make_student_windows(df_10hz, yemo, ydom, seq_length=SEQ_LENGTH):
  usable_rows = (len(df_10hz) // seq_length) * seq_length
  df_10hz = df_10hz.iloc[:usable_rows]
  window = df_10hz.iloc[start:start + seq_length]
  X_eye.append(window[EYE_COLS].to_numpy(dtype=np.float32))
  X_head.append(window[HEAD_COLS].to_numpy(dtype=np.float32))
  X_hand.append(window[HAND_COLS].to_numpy(dtype=np.float32))

CELL 25
  def build_expert(input_tensor, name, units=64, dropout=0.25):
  x = layers.Conv1D(units, 3, padding="same", activation="relu", name=f"{name}_conv1")(input_tensor)
  x = layers.BatchNormalization(name=f"{name}_bn1")(x)
  x = layers.Conv1D(units, 3, padding="same", activation="relu", name=f"{name}_conv2")(x)
  x = layers.GlobalAveragePooling1D(name=f"{name}_pool")(x)
  x = layers.Dropout(dropout, name=f"{name}_dropout")(x)
  x = layers.Dense(units, activation="relu", name=f"{name}_dense")(x)
  def build_student_moe():
  eye_input = layers.Input(shape=(SEQ_LENGTH, len(EYE_COLS)), name="eye_input")
  head_input = layers.Input(shape=(SEQ_LENGTH, len(HEAD_COLS)), name="head_input")
  hand_input = layers.Input(shape=(SEQ_LENGTH, len(HAND_COLS)), name="hand_input")
  eye_expert = build_expert(eye_input, "eye_expert")
  head_expert = build_expert(head_input, "head_expert")
  hand_expert = build_expert(hand_input, "hand_expert")
  merged = layers.Concatenate(axis=-1, name="student_signal_sequence")(
  shared_expert = build_expert(merged, "shared_expert")
  experts = layers.Concatenate(axis=1, name="expert_stack")([
  layers.Reshape((1, 64), name="eye_expert_axis")(eye_expert),
  layers.Reshape((1, 64), name="head_expert_axis")(head_expert),
  layers.Reshape((1, 64), name="hand_expert_axis")(hand_expert),
  layers.Reshape((1, 64), name="shared_expert_axis")(shared_expert),
  gate_features = layers.Concatenate(name="gate_features")(
  [eye_expert, head_expert, hand_expert, shared_expert]
  gate = layers.Dense(64, activation="relu", name="gate_hidden")(gate_features)
  gate = layers.Dense(4, activation="softmax", name="expert_gate")(gate)
  moe = layers.Dot(axes=(1, 1), name="moe_weighted_sum")([gate, experts])
  x = layers.Dropout(0.3, name="moe_dropout")(moe)
  x = layers.Dense(128, activation="relu", name="classifier_hidden")(x)
  emotion_output = layers.Dense(len(le.classes_), activation="softmax", name="emotion_output")(x)
  model = models.Model(
  inputs=[eye_input, head_input, hand_input],
  outputs=emotion_output,
  name="student_MoE_eye_head_hand",
  optimizer=tf.keras.optimizers.Adam(learning_rate=1e-3),
  loss="sparse_categorical_crossentropy",
  metrics=["accuracy"],
  student_model = build_student_moe()
  student_model.summary()

  def downsample_60hz_to_10hz(df, feature_cols):
  df = df[feature_cols].apply(pd.to_numeric, errors="coerce")
  df = df.replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan)
  df = df.ffill().bfill().fillna(0.0)
  usable_rows = (len(df) // DOWNSAMPLE_FACTOR) * DOWNSAMPLE_FACTOR
  df = df.iloc[:usable_rows]
  df_10hz = (
  df.groupby(np.arange(len(df)) // DOWNSAMPLE_FACTOR)
  return df_10hz

CELL 31
  def build_expert(input_tensor, name, units=64, dropout=0.25):
  x = layers.Conv1D(units, 3, padding="same", activation="relu", name=f"{name}_conv1")(input_tensor)
  x = layers.BatchNormalization(name=f"{name}_bn1")(x)
  x = layers.Conv1D(units, 3, padding="same", activation="relu", name=f"{name}_conv2")(x)
  x = layers.GlobalAveragePooling1D(name=f"{name}_pool")(x)
  x = layers.Dropout(dropout, name=f"{name}_dropout")(x)
  x = layers.Dense(units, activation="relu", name=f"{name}_dense")(x)
  def build_student_moe():
  eye_input = layers.Input(shape=(SEQ_LENGTH, len(EYE_COLS)), name="eye_input")
  head_input = layers.Input(shape=(SEQ_LENGTH, len(HEAD_COLS)), name="head_input")
  hand_input = layers.Input(shape=(SEQ_LENGTH, len(HAND_COLS)), name="hand_input")
  eye_expert = build_expert(eye_input, "eye_expert")
  head_expert = build_expert(head_input, "head_expert")
  hand_expert = build_expert(hand_input, "hand_expert")
  merged = layers.Concatenate(axis=-1, name="student_signal_sequence")(
  shared_expert = build_expert(merged, "shared_expert")
  experts = layers.Concatenate(axis=1, name="expert_stack")([
  layers.Reshape((1, 64), name="eye_expert_axis")(eye_expert),
  layers.Reshape((1, 64), name="head_expert_axis")(head_expert),
  layers.Reshape((1, 64), name="hand_expert_axis")(hand_expert),
  layers.Reshape((1, 64), name="shared_expert_axis")(shared_expert),
  gate_features = layers.Concatenate(name="gate_features")(
  [eye_expert, head_expert, hand_expert, shared_expert]
  gate = layers.Dense(64, activation="relu", name="gate_hidden")(gate_features)
  gate = layers.Dense(4, activation="softmax", name="expert_gate")(gate)
  moe = layers.Dot(axes=(1, 1), name="moe_weighted_sum")([gate, experts])
  x = layers.Dropout(0.3, name="moe_dropout")(moe)
  x = layers.Dense(128, activation="relu", name="classifier_hidden")(x)
  emotion_output = layers.Dense(len(le.classes_), activation="softmax", name="emotion_output")(x)
  model = models.Model(
  inputs=[eye_input, head_input, hand_input],
  outputs=emotion_output,
  name="student_MoE_eye_head_hand",
  optimizer=tf.keras.optimizers.Adam(learning_rate=1e-3),
  loss="sparse_categorical_crossentropy",
  metrics=["accuracy"],
  student_model = build_student_moe()
  student_model.summary()

  def train_val_test_data_by_subject(
  random_state: int = 42,
  n_splits: int = 10
  Strict subject-exclusive split (participant in exactly one split),
  â‰ˆ70/10/20 by combining 10 StratifiedGroupKFold folds:
  - train: folds 0..6
  - val:   fold 7
  - test:  folds 8..9
  Stratifies by emotion at the file level while grouping by participant.
  Returns: X_train, X_val, X_test, y_train, y_val, y_test (lists)
  all_files = all_files_list(data_type)
  y = np.array([le.transform([e.value])[0] for e in emos], dtype=int)
  y = np.array([str(e.value) for e in emos])
  # groups = subject ids extracted from folder name
  groups = np.array([subject_from_path(p) for p in files])
  # build 10 folds stratified by emotion and grouped by subject
  sgkf = StratifiedGroupKFold(n_splits=n_splits, shuffle=True, random_state=random_state)
  folds = []
  dummy_X = np.zeros((len(y), 1))
  for _, test_idx in sgkf.split(dummy_X, y, groups=groups):
  folds.append(test_idx)
  if len(folds) != n_splits:
  # combine folds: 7 train, 1 val, 2 test
  train_idx = np.concatenate(folds[:7])
  val_idx   = folds[7]
  test_idx  = np.concatenate(folds[8:])
  train_subjects = set(groups[train_idx])
  val_subjects   = set(groups[val_idx])
  test_subjects  = set(groups[test_idx])
  assert train_subjects.isdisjoint(val_subjects)
  assert train_subjects.isdisjoint(test_subjects)
  assert val_subjects.isdisjoint(test_subjects)
  # build split-specific file lists (tuples of (file, emo))
  train_files = [all_files[i] for i in train_idx]
  val_files   = [all_files[i] for i in val_idx]
  test_files  = [all_files[i] for i in test_idx]
  # chunk per split (no leakage)

  def average_every_3_rows(df: pd.DataFrame, AU_NAMES: list) -> pd.DataFrame:
  Average every 3 consecutive rows within each parent_seq group.
  - Expects groups of 30 rows per parent_seq (produces 10 rows per group).

  def _classifier_head(x, num_classes):
  x = layers.Dense(128, activation="relu")(x)
  x = layers.Dropout(0.3)(x)

  def build_dann(input_shape, num_classes, domain_classes=2, lambda_grl=1.0,
  emotion_class_weights=None, domain_class_weights=None):
  inputs = layers.Input(shape=input_shape)
  x = layers.Conv1D(128, 3, padding="same", activation="relu")(inputs)
  x = layers.Conv1D(128, 3, padding="same", activation="relu")(x)
  x = layers.BatchNormalization()(x)
  feat = layers.GlobalAveragePooling1D(name="features")(x)
  y_out = _classifier_head(feat, num_classes)  # name "y"
  grl = GradientReversal(lambda_grl)(feat)
  d = layers.Dense(128, activation="relu")(grl)
  d = layers.Dropout(0.3)(d)
  d_out = layers.Dense(domain_classes, activation="softmax", name="d")(d)
  model = models.Model(inputs, [y_out, d_out], name="DANN")
  if emotion_class_weights is None:
  emotion_weights = tf.ones((num_classes,), dtype=tf.float32)
  emotion_weights = tf.constant(
  [emotion_class_weights.get(i, 1.0) for i in range(num_classes)],
  dtype=tf.float32
  if domain_class_weights is None:
  domain_weights = tf.ones((domain_classes,), dtype=tf.float32)
  domain_weights = tf.constant(
  [domain_class_weights.get(i, 1.0) for i in range(domain_classes)],
  dtype=tf.float32
  def weighted_emotion_loss(y_true, y_pred):
  y_true_one_hot = tf.one_hot(tf.cast(y_true, tf.int32), depth=num_classes)
  sample_weights = tf.gather(emotion_weights, tf.cast(y_true, tf.int32))
  cce = tf.keras.losses.categorical_crossentropy(y_true_one_hot, y_pred)
  weighted_loss = sample_weights * cce
  def weighted_domain_loss(d_true, d_pred):
  d_true_one_hot = tf.one_hot(tf.cast(d_true, tf.int32), depth=domain_classes)
  sample_weights = tf.gather(domain_weights, tf.cast(d_true, tf.int32))
  cce = tf.keras.losses.categorical_crossentropy(d_true_one_hot, d_pred)
  weighted_loss = sample_weights * cce
  optimizer=tf.keras.optimizers.SGD(1e-3),
  loss={"y": weighted_emotion_loss, "d": weighted_domain_loss},
  metrics={"y": ["accuracy"], "d": ["accuracy"]},
  loss_weights={"y": 1.0, "d": 1.0}

  def _tcn_block(x, filters, kernel_size, dilation_rate, dropout=0.2):
  res = x
  y = layers.Conv1D(filters, kernel_size, padding="causal",
  dilation_rate=dilation_rate)(x)
  y = layers.BatchNormalization()(y)
  y = layers.ReLU()(y)
  y = layers.Dropout(dropout)(y)
  y = layers.Conv1D(filters, kernel_size, padding="causal",
  dilation_rate=dilation_rate)(y)
  y = layers.BatchNormalization()(y)
  res = layers.Conv1D(filters, 1, padding="same")(res)
  res = layers.BatchNormalization()(res)
  y = layers.Add()([y, res])
  y = layers.ReLU()(y)
  def _classifier_head(x, num_classes):
  x = layers.Dense(128, activation="relu")(x)
  x = layers.Dropout(0.3)(x)
  return layers.Dense(num_classes, activation="softmax", name="y")(x)
  def build_dann_tcn(input_shape, num_classes, domain_classes=2, lambda_grl=0.5,
  emotion_class_weights=None, domain_class_weights=None):
  inputs = layers.Input(shape=input_shape)
  x = _tcn_block(inputs, filters=128, kernel_size=3, dilation_rate=1, dropout=0.2)
  x = _tcn_block(x,      filters=128, kernel_size=3, dilation_rate=2, dropout=0.2)
  x = _tcn_block(x,      filters=128, kernel_size=3, dilation_rate=4, dropout=0.2)
  feat = layers.GlobalAveragePooling1D(name="features")(x)
  y_out = _classifier_head(feat, num_classes)
  grl = GradientReversal(lambda_grl)(feat)
  d = layers.Dense(128, activation="relu")(grl)
  d = layers.Dropout(0.3)(d)
  d_out = layers.Dense(domain_classes, activation="softmax", name="d")(d)
  model = models.Model(inputs, [y_out, d_out], name="DANN_TCN")
  if emotion_class_weights is None:
  emotion_weights = tf.ones((num_classes,), dtype=tf.float32)
  emotion_weights = tf.constant(
  [emotion_class_weights.get(i, 1.0) for i in range(num_classes)], dtype=tf.float32
  if domain_class_weights is None:
  domain_weights = tf.ones((domain_classes,), dtype=tf.float32)
  domain_weights = tf.constant(
  [domain_class_weights.get(i, 1.0) for i in range(domain_classes)], dtype=tf.float32
  def weighted_emotion_loss(y_true, y_pred):
  y_true_oh = tf.one_hot(tf.cast(y_true, tf.int32), depth=num_classes)
  sw = tf.gather(emotion_weights, tf.cast(y_true, tf.int32))
  cce = tf.keras.losses.categorical_crossentropy(y_true_oh, y_pred)
  def weighted_domain_loss(d_true, d_pred):
  d_true_oh = tf.one_hot(tf.cast(d_true, tf.int32), depth=domain_classes)
  sw = tf.gather(domain_weights, tf.cast(d_true, tf.int32))
  cce = tf.keras.losses.categorical_crossentropy(d_true_oh, d_pred)
  model.compile(optimizer=tf.keras.optimizers.SGD(1e-3),
  loss={"y": weighted_emotion_loss, "d": weighted_domain_loss},
  metrics={"y": ["accuracy"], "d": ["accuracy"]},
  loss_weights={"y": 1.0, "d": 1.0})

CELL 92
  # ---- TCN expert blocks ----
  def _tcn_block(x, filters, kernel_size, dilation_rate, dropout=0.2, name=None):
  res = x
  y = layers.Conv1D(filters, kernel_size, padding="causal",
  dilation_rate=dilation_rate, name=None if name is None else name+"_c1")(x)
  y = layers.BatchNormalization(name=None if name is None else name+"_bn1")(y)
  y = layers.ReLU(name=None if name is None else name+"_relu1")(y)
  y = layers.Dropout(dropout, name=None if name is None else name+"_do1")(y)
  y = layers.Conv1D(filters, kernel_size, padding="causal",
  dilation_rate=dilation_rate, name=None if name is None else name+"_c2")(y)
  y = layers.BatchNormalization(name=None if name is None else name+"_bn2")(y)
  res = layers.Conv1D(filters, 1, padding="same", name=None if name is None else name+"_proj")(res)
  res = layers.BatchNormalization(name=None if name is None else name+"_proj_bn")(res)
  y = layers.Add(name=None if name is None else name+"_add")([y, res])
  y = layers.ReLU(name=None if name is None else name+"_relu2")(y)
  def build_expert_tcn(x, name_prefix="exp"):
  y = _tcn_block(x, 128, 3, 1, 0.2, name=name_prefix+"_tcn1")
  y = _tcn_block(y, 128, 3, 2, 0.2, name=name_prefix+"_tcn2")
  y = _tcn_block(y, 128, 3, 4, 0.2, name=name_prefix+"_tcn3")
  y = layers.GlobalAveragePooling1D(name=name_prefix+"_gap")(y)
  # ---- Top-2 gate as a Keras Layer ----
  class Top2Gate(layers.Layer):
  def __init__(self, num_experts=3, gate_hidden=128, tau_init=2.0,
  lb_coef=0.02, ent_coef=0.01, name="gate", **kwargs):
  self.num_experts = num_experts
  self.gate_hidden = gate_hidden
  self.tau = tf.Variable(tau_init, dtype=tf.float32, trainable=False, name=f"{name}_tau")
  self.d1 = layers.Dense(gate_hidden, activation="relu", name=f"{name}_dense1")
  self.d2 = layers.Dense(num_experts, activation=None, name=f"{name}_logits")
  def call(self, gate_in, training=None):
  logits = self.d2(self.d1(gate_in))           # (B, E)
  w_soft = tf.nn.softmax(logits / self.tau, -1)  # (B, E)
  idx = top2.indices            # (B, 2)
  vals = tf.nn.softmax(top2.values, -1)  # re-norm top-2
  B = tf.shape(w_soft)[0]; E = tf.shape(w_soft)[1]
  row_ids = tf.repeat(tf.range(B), repeats=2)
  col_ids = tf.reshape(idx, (-1,))
  scatter_idx = tf.stack([row_ids, col_ids], axis=1)   # (B*2, 2)
  scatter_vals = tf.reshape(vals, (-1,))
  w_sparse = tf.scatter_nd(scatter_idx, scatter_vals, (B, self.num_experts))  # (B,E)
  mean_usage = tf.reduce_mean(w_sparse, axis=0)
  uniform = tf.fill([self.num_experts], 1.0 / tf.cast(self.num_experts, tf.float32))
  lb_loss = tf.reduce_sum(tf.square(mean_usage - uniform))
  ent = -tf.reduce_mean(tf.reduce_sum(w_soft * tf.math.log(tf.clip_by_value(w_soft, 1e-8, 1.0)), axis=-1))
  # ---- Temperature scheduler ----
  class GateTemperatureScheduler(callbacks.Callback):
  def __init__(self, gate_layer, start_tau=2.0, end_tau=0.7, anneal_epochs=20):
  self.gate = gate_layer
  self.anneal_epochs = max(1, int(anneal_epochs))
  def on_epoch_begin(self, epoch, logs=None):
  p = min(1.0, epoch / self.anneal_epochs)
  tau = self.start_tau + p * (self.end_tau - self.start_tau)
  self.gate.tau.assign(tau)
  K.set_value(self.gate.tau, tau)
  if epoch % 5 == 0:
  print(f"[GateTemperatureScheduler] epoch={epoch} tau={tau:.3f}")

  df.groupby(np.arange(len(df)) // DOWNSAMPLE_FACTOR)
